# Native finite commitments. Shipping uses send_equipment, which transfers
# actual donor inventory; this file never creates equipment stockpile gifts.
# Single active commitment and explicit peer flags make processing idempotent.
terra_contract_snapshot_faction_effect = {
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_signed_in_faction
    if = {
        limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
        set_country_flag = terra_contract_signed_in_faction
        faction_leader = {
            PREV = { terra_contract_mark_role_effect = { PARTNER = PREV ROLE = faction_anchor } }
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_clear_local_pending_effect = {
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_pending
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_pending_aid_donor
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_pending_aid_client
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_pending_defender
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_pending_protected
}
terra_contract_cancel_pending_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { terra_contract_has_role_with = { PARTNER = FROM ROLE = pending_with } }
        terra_contract_clear_pending_peer_effect = { PARTNER = FROM }
        terra_contract_clear_local_pending_effect = yes
    }
    FROM = {
        if = {
            limit = { terra_contract_has_role_with = { PARTNER = ROOT ROLE = pending_with } }
            terra_contract_clear_pending_peer_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT }
            terra_contract_clear_local_pending_effect = yes
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_begin_pending_effect = {
    set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending value = 1 days = 90 }
    set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_offer_cooldown value = 1 days = 180 }
    terra_contract_mark_pending_peer_effect = { PARTNER = FROM }
    FROM = {
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending value = 1 days = 90 }
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_incoming_cooldown value = 1 days = 90 }
        terra_contract_mark_pending_peer_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT }
    }
}
terra_contract_offer_aid_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { terra_contract_open_from = yes has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 299 } }
        terra_contract_begin_pending_effect = yes
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_aid_donor value = 1 days = 90 }
        FROM = {
            set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_aid_client value = 1 days = 90 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.1 }
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_offer_defense_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            terra_contract_open_from = yes
            NOT = { has_guaranteed = FROM }
            DIPLOMACY_GUARANTEE_ENABLE_TRIGGER = yes
        }
        terra_contract_begin_pending_effect = yes
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_defender value = 1 days = 90 }
        FROM = {
            set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_protected value = 1 days = 90 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.20 }
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_sign_aid_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { terra_contract_aid_donor_from = yes has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > $MIN_STOCK$ } }
        terra_contract_cancel_pending_effect = yes
        set_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_mark_role_effect = { PARTNER = FROM ROLE = aid_donor }
        terra_contract_mark_timed_role_effect = { PARTNER = FROM ROLE = aid_wait DAYS = 14 }
        if = { limit = { always = $SMALL$ } set_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small }
        terra_contract_snapshot_faction_effect = yes
        FROM = {
            set_country_flag = terra_contract_active
            terra_contract_mark_role_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT ROLE = aid_receiver }
            terra_contract_snapshot_faction_effect = yes
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.5 }
        }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.40 days = 14 }
    }
    else = { terra_contract_cancel_pending_effect = yes }
}
terra_contract_sign_defense_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { terra_contract_defense_donor_from = yes }
        terra_contract_cancel_pending_effect = yes
        give_guarantee = FROM
        set_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_mark_role_effect = { PARTNER = FROM ROLE = defender }
        terra_contract_mark_role_effect = { PARTNER = FROM ROLE = guarantee_owned }
        terra_contract_mark_timed_role_effect = { PARTNER = FROM ROLE = defense_term DAYS = $DAYS$ }
        terra_contract_snapshot_faction_effect = yes
        FROM = {
            set_country_flag = terra_contract_active
            terra_contract_mark_role_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT ROLE = protected }
            terra_contract_snapshot_faction_effect = yes
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.25 }
        }
    }
    else = { terra_contract_cancel_pending_effect = yes }
}

terra_contract_close_pair_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_$TARGET$ has_country_flag = terra_contract_guarantee_owned_$TARGET$ }
        if = {
            limit = { has_guaranteed = $TARGET$ }
            diplomatic_relation = { country = $TARGET$ relation = guarantee active = no }
        }
    }
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_guarantee_owned_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_wait_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_grace_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_due_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_term_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_window_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_honored_$TARGET$
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small
    clr_country_flag = terra_contract_active
    terra_contract_clear_local_snapshot_effect = yes
    $TARGET$ = { terra_contract_release_client_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT } }
}
terra_contract_record_breach_effect = {
    set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_recent_default value = 1 days = 365 }
    terra_state_record_breach_effect = yes
    $TARGET$ = {
        terra_dip_change_relation_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT TRUST = -20 GRIEVANCE = $GRIEF$ }
        add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = terra_contract_breached days = 365 }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.70 }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.71 }
    }
}
terra_contract_deliver_aid_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_$TARGET$
            terra_contract_pair_valid = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
            has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > $MIN_STOCK$ }
        }
        # Clear the unique active shipment before the conserved native transfer.
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
        send_equipment = { type = infantry_equipment amount = $AMOUNT$ target = $TARGET$ old_prioritised = yes }
        terra_state_record_fulfillment_effect = yes
        terra_dip_change_relation_effect = { PARTNER = $TARGET$ TRUST = 5 GRIEVANCE = -1 }
        $TARGET$ = {
            terra_dip_change_relation_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT TRUST = 12 GRIEVANCE = -4 }
            add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = terra_contract_delivered days = 180 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.60 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.61 }
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_honor_defense_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_honored_$TARGET$ } }
        set_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_honored_$TARGET$
        terra_state_record_fulfillment_effect = yes
        terra_dip_change_relation_effect = { PARTNER = $TARGET$ TRUST = 4 GRIEVANCE = -1 }
        $TARGET$ = {
            terra_dip_change_relation_effect = { PARTNER = ROOT TRUST = 18 GRIEVANCE = -6 }
            add_opinion_modifier = { target = ROOT modifier = terra_contract_defense_honored days = 180 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.65 }
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_invalid_pair_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            terra_dip_sovereign = yes
            $TARGET$ = { terra_dip_sovereign = yes }
            OR = {
                has_war_with = $TARGET$
                NOT = { terra_contract_faction_unchanged = yes }
            }
        }
        terra_contract_record_breach_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ GRIEF = 15 }
    }
    terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
    country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }
}
terra_contract_process_aid_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { terra_contract_pair_valid = { TARGET = $TARGET$ } } }
        terra_contract_invalid_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_due_$TARGET$
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_grace_$TARGET$ }
        }
        # A weekly fallback cannot reward stock replenished after the deadline.
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
        terra_contract_record_breach_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ GRIEF = 15 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_wait_$TARGET$ } }
        if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    AND = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 149 } }
                    AND = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small } has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 299 } }
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small }
                terra_contract_deliver_aid_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ AMOUNT = 150 MIN_STOCK = 149 }
            }
            else = { terra_contract_deliver_aid_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ AMOUNT = 300 MIN_STOCK = 299 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_due_$TARGET$ } }
            set_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_due_$TARGET$
            set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_aid_grace_$TARGET$ value = 1 days = 7 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.41 days = 7 }
            country_event = { id = terra_promises.66 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_grace_$TARGET$ } }
            terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
            terra_contract_record_breach_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ GRIEF = 15 }
        }
    }
}
terra_contract_process_defense_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { terra_contract_pair_valid = { TARGET = $TARGET$ } } }
        terra_contract_invalid_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_guaranteed = $TARGET$ } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
        terra_contract_record_breach_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ GRIEF = 25 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_term_$TARGET$ }
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$ }
        }
        # A new war after term expiry cannot create or extend a fresh duty.
        terra_contract_honor_defense_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { $TARGET$ = { has_defensive_war = yes } }
        if = {
            limit = { terra_contract_aiding_defense_of = { TARGET = $TARGET$ } }
            terra_contract_honor_defense_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
            clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$
            clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_window_$TARGET$
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$ } }
            set_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$
            set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_defense_window_$TARGET$ value = 1 days = 14 }
            $TARGET$ = { country_event = { id = terra_promises.67 } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_window_$TARGET$ } }
            terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
            terra_contract_record_breach_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ GRIEF = 25 }
        }
    }
    else = {
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_window_$TARGET$
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_$TARGET$
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_term_$TARGET$ }
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defense_call_$TARGET$ }
        }
        terra_contract_honor_defense_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = $TARGET$ }
    }
}


terra_contract_mark_role_effect = {

        set_country_flag = terra_contract_$ROLE$_AGI

terra_contract_mark_pending_peer_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { $PARTNER$ = { tag = AGI } }
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_with_AGI value = 1 days = 90 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { $PARTNER$ = { tag = BLV } }
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_with_BLV value = 1 days = 90 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { $PARTNER$ = { tag = CLM } }
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_pending_with_CLM value = 1 days = 90 }
    }
    if = {

    if = {
        limit = { $PARTNER$ = { tag = IBR } }

    }
    if = {
        limit = { $PARTNER$ = { tag = KJG } }

        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { terra_contract_pair_valid = { TARGET = AGI } } }
            terra_contract_invalid_pair_effect = { TARGET = AGI }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 149 } }
            terra_contract_deliver_aid_effect = { TARGET = AGI AMOUNT = 150 MIN_STOCK = 149 }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_small } has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 299 } }

        }

terra_contract_cancel_from_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { tag = AGI } OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_AGI has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_AGI } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = AGI }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { tag = BLV } OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_BLV has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_BLV } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = BLV }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { tag = CLM } OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_CLM has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_CLM } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = CLM }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { tag = HGS } OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_HGS has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_HGS } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = HGS }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { tag = HGN } OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_HGN has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_HGN } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = HGN }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { tag = HSS } OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_HSS has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_HSS } }
        terra_contract_close_pair_effect = { TARGET = HSS }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.73 }

terra_contract_withdraw_from_effect = {
    if = {

# Callable by root on_weekly in country ROOT=THIS context.
terra_contract_weekly_effect = {
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_AGI } terra_contract_process_aid_effect = { TARGET = AGI } }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_AGI } terra_contract_process_defense_effect = { TARGET = AGI } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_AGI has_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_AGI }
            OR = {
                AGI = { NOT = { terra_dip_sovereign = yes } }
                AGI = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_active } }
            }
        }
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_AGI
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_AGI
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_clear_local_snapshot_effect = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_BLV } terra_contract_process_aid_effect = { TARGET = BLV } }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_BLV } terra_contract_process_defense_effect = { TARGET = BLV } }

        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_clear_local_snapshot_effect = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_D48 } terra_contract_process_aid_effect = { TARGET = D48 } }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_D48 } terra_contract_process_defense_effect = { TARGET = D48 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_D48 has_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_D48 }
            OR = {
                D48 = { NOT = { terra_dip_sovereign = yes } }
                D48 = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_active } }
            }
        }
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_D48
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_D48
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_clear_local_snapshot_effect = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_D49 } terra_contract_process_aid_effect = { TARGET = D49 } }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_D49 } terra_contract_process_defense_effect = { TARGET = D49 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_D49 has_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_D49 }
            OR = {
                D49 = { NOT = { terra_dip_sovereign = yes } }
                D49 = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_active } }
            }
        }
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_D49
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_D49
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_clear_local_snapshot_effect = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_donor_D50 } terra_contract_process_aid_effect = { TARGET = D50 } }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_defender_D50 } terra_contract_process_defense_effect = { TARGET = D50 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_D50 has_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_D50 }
            OR = {
                D50 = { NOT = { terra_dip_sovereign = yes } }
                D50 = { NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_active } }
            }
        }
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_aid_receiver_D50
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_protected_D50
        clr_country_flag = terra_contract_active
        terra_contract_clear_local_snapshot_effect = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_ai = yes
            terra_contract_free = yes
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_offer_cooldown }
            NOT = { has_country_flag = terra_contract_search_cooldown }
            has_political_power > 24
            has_equipment = { infantry_equipment > 999 }
        }
        set_country_flag = { flag = terra_contract_search_cooldown value = 1 days = 30 }
        country_event = { id = terra_promises.100 }
    }
}
